# Skills · M · part 24

937 entries, memory-impact-analysis to memory-to-skill. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **memory-impact-analysis**: Use when the user wants to know what will break if they change something, or needs safety analysis before editing code. Examples: "is it safe to change X?", "what depends on this?", "what will break?", "show me the blast radius". Also compose with PR review — run this per touched symbol after `gh pr diff`. ([unknown-studio-dev/hoangsa](https://github.com/unknown-studio-dev/hoangsa/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/memory-impact-analysis) · ★26 · 2 copies)
- **memory-import**: Bulk-import an external knowledge base into Prismer memory via the three-layer import pipeline (07 §3.1). Use when the user asks to import/migrate documents, a knowledge base, or notes into workspace memory. Layer 1 lands raw documents (zero LLM, instantly searchable); Layer 2 is YOUR distillation work — you read the landed documents and write candidate pages; Layer 3 commits candidates through the memory evolution gate. Distinct from the native memory_write tool face (that is for single, in-conversation writes). ([Prismer-AI/PrismerCloud](https://github.com/Prismer-AI/PrismerCloud/tree/HEAD/sdk/cloud/catalog/skills/memory-import) · ★1.4k)
- **memory-import**: Inherit memories from another workspace. Reads .claude/memory/ from a source path and imports its memories into the current workspace under .claude/memory/inherited/&lt;source-name&gt;/. Tagged with provenance so the user can tell where each came from. ([viknesh20-20/claude-code-tool-kit](https://github.com/viknesh20-20/claude-code-tool-kit/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/memory-import) · ★7)
- **memory-import**: 【必須】他のcc-memoryインスタンスが書き出したexportバンドルを取り込む。「このバンドルを取り込んで」「importして」「バンドルを読み込んで」などバンドルパスの提示を伴う発話で発動。このスキルを経由せずにimport_bundleを直接呼んではいけない。 ([isizono/calm](https://github.com/isizono/calm/tree/HEAD/skills/memory-import) · ★3)
- **memory-import**: This skill should be used when the user asks to "importer un doc dans la mémoire", "normaliser ce document en mémoire", "transformer ce doc en faits", "ajouter de la doc au vault", "import doc to memory", or "/memory-import". It normalizes a raw document into atomic memory facts (frontmatter + one fact per file) in the vault working copy, ready for /memory-promote. ([Manguet/shared-memory](https://github.com/Manguet/shared-memory/tree/HEAD/skills/memory-import) · ★1)
- **memory-import**: Import a validated sharedMemory snapshot using the memory_import MCP tool. Restore, migrate, or merge memory graph data containing project-specific and general entries. ([pruthuraj/sharedMemory](https://github.com/pruthuraj/sharedMemory/tree/HEAD/skills/memory-import))
- **memory-import-guide**: Preview and import a user-supplied conversation export from ChatGPT, Claude, Gemini, Copilot, or Perplexity into StudioMeyer Memory when the user requests that import. ([studiomeyer-io/studiomeyer-marketplace](https://github.com/studiomeyer-io/studiomeyer-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/studiomeyer-memory/skills/memory-import-guide) · ★2)
- **memory-import-sanitizer**: Use this skill when importing ChatGPT or Claude exports, old Notion databases, local notes, or messy knowledge dumps into a durable knowledge system after redaction, deduplication, and classification. Do not use for live project planning, raw unredacted client data, or content that should remain out of Notion. ([amnafarzy/ravie](https://github.com/amnafarzy/ravie/tree/HEAD/archive/skills/memory-import-sanitizer) · ★3)
- **memory-index**: Register a memory scope's `_memory/&lt;type&gt;/` folders and `notes/` with qmd as collections, and reindex after records land. Use when the user says "index my memory", "set up qmd for this scope", "register this scope with qmd", wants semantic search over memory, or says "refresh the memory index", and when a hook or another skill needs the index current after a write. Never writes or edits records: to groom an inbox into notes and records, use inbox-to-memory. ([kendrick/skills](https://github.com/kendrick/skills/tree/HEAD/memory-index))
- **memory-index-compact**: Compact verbose memory index / registry files to pointer-only form. Activate when an index file (index.md, index-decisions.md, or any sibling registry table) breaches the file-size budget OR shows substance-duplication drift. Distinct from memory-compact (which targets content categories) and memory-archive-superseded (which migrates superseded rows to archive). ([digipulse-engineering/GAAI-framework](https://github.com/digipulse-engineering/GAAI-framework/tree/HEAD/.gaai/core/skills/cross/memory-index-compact) · ★161)

- **memory-keeper**: Keeps your memory current with the least effort from you. Two triggers. (1) Capture - at the end of a turn or on "remember this", it notices a durable new fact and proposes a memory update. (2) Context - while reading a source (meeting, chat, email), it notices things named but unseen, and drift against what memory already says, and queues or surfaces them. Propose-only; it never writes to memory without your yes. Triggers on "remember this", "capture that", "what am I missing", "catch up your context", or automatically end-of-turn when confidence is high. - **memory-systems-zh**: Agent 记忆系统设计、跨会话知识保留、长期记忆、实体追踪与身份一致、时间有效性（事实会过期）、向量检索 vs 知识图谱 vs 时态知识图谱、记忆整理与合并、记忆框架选型（Mem0 / Zep-Graphiti / Letta / LangMem / Cognee / 文件系统）、记忆基准（LoCoMo / LongMemEval / DMR）。当用户说「Agent 怎么记住上次会话的事」「用什么记忆框架」「同一个人在不同对话里怎么保持一致」「过期的记忆污染了回答」「要不要上知识图谱」时使用。核心：记忆是从易失的上下文窗口到持久存储的光谱，默认选满足检索需求的最浅一层（先文件系统原型，再向量+元数据，再时态图谱，最后自管理记忆）；检索策略按查询形状匹配（语义 / 实体遍历 / 时间过滤 / 混合）；周期性整理——失效但不删除；记忆按需即时加载并放在注意力偏好位置；为检索失败设计兜底。附实现参考（向量库、属性图、时态图、整理器）。改编自 Agent Skills for Context Engineering 的 memory-systems（MIT）。 ([po-et/workbuddy-skills](https://github.com/po-et/workbuddy-skills/tree/HEAD/ported/skills/memory-systems-zh))

- **memory-tidy**: Claude Code 자동 메모리(프로젝트별 `memory/` 폴더와 `~/.claude/global-memory/`)를 정리하고, 어느 프로젝트에나 맞는 항목을 전역으로 승격한다. 사용자가 "메모리 정리해줘", "메모리 점검", "메모리에 오래된 거 있나", "이거 전역으로 올려"라고 하거나, 작업 목표를 끝내고 진행 상태 파일(STATE.md 등)을 비우기 직전이면 발동한다. 메모리 정리 요청에는 anthropic-skills:consolidate-memory 대신 이 스킬을 쓴다(삭제 전 승인과 승격 단계가 있다). 또한 "이 스킬 의도대로 작동해?", "이 스킬 잘 되고 있어?"처럼 스킬 자체의 효과를 점검해 달라는 요청에도 발동하며, 그때는 같은 폴더의 `RATIONALE.md`를 읽고 이번 세션·기존 기록과 대조한다. - **memory-tidy**: Keep Claude Code's always-loaded MEMORY.md index healthy — trim over-length entries, move done work to the archive, fold domains into hub files, and give orphaned notes a pointer so they stay findable. Triggers on "/memory-tidy", "tidy memory", "my memory index is huge", "MEMORY.md is over budget", "orphaned memory notes", "clean up memory". Also run it yourself when the memory guard reports RED or the index approaches its budget. Do NOT touch an index another machine owns — ownership is declared in memory_scope.json, never guessed. - **memory-tidy**: プロジェクトの auto memory（MEMORY.md ＋ トピック/タスクファイル群）を整理・圧縮する。完了タスクの進行中からの削除と完了記録化、実装完了で肥大したエントリの task-*.md への退避と要約化、再利用知見のナレッジファイル昇格、プロジェクト非依存の普遍ルールの CLAUDE.md への昇格提案、空ファイル・壊れリンク・重複索引の掃除、行数/容量の検証までを一貫して行う。ユーザーが「メモリ整理して」「MEMORY を整理」「メモリが肥大化した」「MEMORY.md が大きくなった/重い」「メモリの棚卸し」「auto memory を整理」「定期メンテ」と言ったとき、または MEMORY.md が運用ルールの行数上限を超えていることに気づいたときに使う。整理は削除・圧縮を伴うので、必ずプロジェクトの運用ルールに従い、範囲をユーザーに確認してから実行する。 ([ymegane/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/ymegane/claude-code-plugins/tree/HEAD/memory-tidy/skills/memory-tidy))
